           .or_else(|| sugar_rs::optim::trainer::guess_images_dir_from_sparse(&scene))\n            .expect(\"Missing --images and couldn't guess images dir\");\n        (scene, images_dir)\n    };\n\n    // Determine output directory: use --out-dir if specified, otherwise create timestamped directory\n    let final_out_dir = if let Some(dir) = out_dir {\n        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).ok();\n        dir\n    } else {\n        let preset = preset_name.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"custom\");\n        create_run_directory(preset)\n            .expect(\"Failed to create run directory\")\n    };\n\n    // Save run metadata (seed will be updated after training)\n    let all_args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().collect();\n    save_run_metadata(&final_out_dir, &all_args, None)\n        .unwrap_or_else(|e| eprintln!(\"Warning: Failed to save metadata: {}\", e));\n\n    // Auto-calculate downsample factor if not explicitly set\n    if !downsample_explicit && Some(ext) = direct.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) {\n                                let ext_lower = ext.to_ascii_lowercase();\n                                if ext_lower == \"jpg\" || ext_lower == \"jpeg\" {\n                                    let mut png_path = direct.clone();\n                                    png_path.set_extension(\"png\");\n    jpg_path.set_extension(\"jpg\");\n                                    candidates.push(jpg_path);\n                                    let mut jpeg_path = direct.clone();\n                                    jpeg_path.set_extension(\"jpeg\");\n                                    candidates.push(jpeg_path);\n                                }\n                            } else {\n                                let mut png_path = direct.clone();\n                                png_path.set_extension(\"png\");\n                                candidates.push(png_path);\n                                let mut jpg_path = direct.clone();\n                                jpg_path.set_extension(\"jpg\");\n                                candidates.push(jpg_path);\n                                let mut jpeg_path = direct.clone();\n                                jpeg_path.set_extension(\"jpeg\");\n                                candidates.push(jpeg_path);\n                            }\n                            candidates.into_iter().find(|p| p.exists()).unwrap_or(direct)\n                        }\n                    };\n\n                    let max_buffer_size = auto_downsample::get_gpu_max_buffer_size();\n                    match auto_downsample::determine_auto_downsample(&first_image_path, max_buffer_size) {\n                        Ok((auto_downsample_factor, width, height)) => {\n                            // Only warn if we're actually downsampling\n                            if auto_downsample_factor < 1.0 {\n                                auto_downsample::print_auto_downsample_warning(\n                                    width,\n                                    height,\n                                    max_buffer_size / (1024 * 1024),\n                                    auto_downsample_factor,\n                                );\n                            }\n                            downsample = auto_downsample_factor;\n                        }\n                        Err(e) => {\n                            eprintln!(\"Warning: {}\", e);\n                            eprintln!(\"Using default downsample factor: {}\", downsample);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            Err(e) => {\n                eprintln!(\"Warning: Failed to load COLMAP scene for auto-downsample: {}\", e);\n                eprintln!(\"Using default downsample factor: {}\", downsample);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Derive dataset root from scene path before scene is moved (scene is sparse/0, so go up two levels)\n    let dataset_path = scene.parent()\n        .and_then(|p| p.parent())\n        .map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string())\n        .unwrap_or_default();\n\n    if multiview {\n        let cfg = sugar_rs::optim::trainer::MultiViewTrainConfig {\n            sparse_dir: scene,\n            images_dir,\n     use_gpu,\n            csv_output_path: Some(final_out_dir.join(\"metrics.csv\")),\n            out_dir: final_out_dir.clone(),\n            disable_sh,\n        };\n\n        let out = eprintln!(\"Saved `{}`\", rendered_path.display());\n        eprintln!(\"Saved `{}`\", target_path.display());\n\n        if std::env::var(\"SUGAR_DEBUG_TARGET\").is_ok() {\n            let target = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CXmFgviAHWmbGvyxy2R9Nm","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# Training Quality Troubleshooting Guide\n     2→\n     3→This document captures lessons learned from debugging training failures and provides recommendations for stable, high-quality 3D Gaussian Splatting training.\n     4→\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→## Table of Contents\n     8→\n     9→1. [Common Failure Modes](#common-failure-modes)\n    10→2. [Root Causes Identified](#root-causes-identified)\n    11→3. [Solutions Implemented](#solutions-implemented)\n    12→4. [Recommended Configurations](#recommended-configurations)\n    13→5. [Troubleshooting Checklist](#troubleshooting-checklist)\n    14→6. [GPU-Specific Considerations](#gpu-specific-considerations)\n    15→\n    16→---\n    17→\n    18→## Common Failure Modes\n    19→\n    20→### 1. Solid Color Rendering (Bad Train/Test Split)\n    21→\n    22→**Symptoms:**\n    23→- Rendered images are solid color (single RGB value)\n    24→- File sizes: 6-20 KB (compressed solid color)\n    25→- PSNR degrading or minimal improvement\n    26→- Occurs immediately or within first 1000 iterations\n    27→\n    28→**Example metrics:**\n    29→```\n    30→Initial PSNR: 12.10 dB\n    31→Final PSNR: 11.68 dB (degrading)\n    32→File size: 6.1 KB (solid color)\n    33→```\n    34→\n    35→**Root cause:** Random seed selected training views with poor geometric coverage (e.g., all from similar angles).\n    36→\n    37→**Solution:** Use seed 123 (proven stable for 20-75 images).\n    38→\n    39→---\n    40→\n    41→### 2. Background Optimizer Divergence\n    42→\n    43→**Symptoms:**\n    44→- Background RGB values exceed 1.0 (physically impossible)\n    45→- PSNR degrading over time\n    46→- File sizes reasonable (200-400 KB) but quality poor\n    47→- Gradual failure over thousands of iterations\n    48→\n    49→**Example metrics:**\n    50→```\n    51→Iteration 1K: bg=(0.46, 0.55, 0.47) PSNR=8.66 dB ✓\n    52→Iteration 2K: bg=(0.86, 1.00, 0.83) PSNR=7.50 dB ⚠️\n    53→Iteration 3K: bg=(0.95, 1.15, 0.84) PSNR=6.52 dB ❌ (green > 1.0!)\n    54→```\n    55→\n    56→**Root cause:** Background learning rate (0.001) too high for large datasets.\n    57→- 20-75 images: ~1,500-7,500 background gradient updates ✓\n    58→- 301 images: ~240,000 background gradient updates ❌ (32× more)\n    59→\n    60→**Solution:** Either:\n    61→- Lower background LR by 10× (0.0001 for 301 images)\n    62→- Disable background learning (`--no-learn-bg`)\n    63→\n    64→---\n    65→\n    66→### 3. Densification Explosion\n    67→\n    68→**Symptoms:**\n    69→- Gaussian count grows exponentially (25K → 400K+)\n    70→- GPU crashes with buffer overflow error\n    71→- gradients exploding (grad_p90 > 1.0)\n    72→- Occurs around iterations 5000-8500\n    73→\n    74→**Example metrics:**\n    75→```\n    76→Iteration 5K: 73,548 Gaussians, grad_p90=0.0007\n    77→Iteration 6K: 102,842 Gaussians, grad_p90=0.2626\n    78→Iteration 7K: 170,640 Gaussians, grad_p90=0.7824\n    79→Iteration 8K: 272,112 Gaussians, grad_p90=1.2017\n    80→Iteration 8.5K: 423,821 Gaussians → GPU CRASH\n    81→```\n    82→\n    83→**Error message:**\n    84→```\n    85→Buffer binding 1 range 135622720 exceeds max_*_buffer_binding_size limit 134217728\n    86→```\n    87→\n    88→**Root cause:** Densification threshold (0.0002) too aggressive for large datasets.\n    89→- More views → more gradient signal → more Gaussians marked for densification\n    90→- Densification cap mechanism can't keep up with explosive growth\n    91→\n    92→**Solution:**\n    93→- Raise densification threshold: 0.0002 → 0.002 (10× higher)\n    94→- Lower max Gaussians cap: 150K → 100K\n    95→- GPU hard cap enforced: 400K max (prevents crashes)\n    96→\n    97→---\n    98→\n    99→### 4. General Optimizer Divergence (Large Datasets)\n   100→\n   101→**Symptoms:**\n   102→- PSNR degrading over time despite reasonable metrics\n   103→- Visual artifacts (black areas, floating Gaussians)\n   104→- File sizes OK, background OK, Gaussian count OK\n   105→- All fixes applied but still failing\n   106→\n   107→**Example metrics (301 images):**\n   108→```\n   109→Iteration 1K: PSNR=7.91 dB, 49,895 Gaussians ✓\n   110→Iteration 3K: PSNR=7.91 dB, 49,880 Gaussians (flat)\n   111→Iteration 5K: PSNR=6.77 dB, 50,787 Gaussians ❌ (degrading)\n   112→Iteration 7K: PSNR=5.38 dB, 61,703 Gaussians ❌ (much worse)\n   113→```\n   114→\n   115→**Root cause:** Learning rates tuned for 20-75 images are too high for 240+ training views.\n   116→\n   117→**Solution:** Limit dataset to 100 images (stay in known-working regime).\n   118→\n   119→---\n   120→\n   121→## Root Causes Identified\n   122→\n   123→### 1. Random Seed Sensitivity\n   124→\n   125→**Problem:** Different random seeds produce dramatically different train/test splits.\n   126→\n   127→**Impact by seed:**\n   128→\n   129→| Seed | 40 images | 75 images | Diagnosis |\n   130→|------|-----------|-----------|-----------|\n   131→| 0    | ❌ -0.42 dB (6KB) | ❌ -1.29 dB (6KB) | Bad view selection |\n   132→| 42   | ❌ -0.31 dB (7KB) | Unknown | Bad view selection |\n   133→| 123  | ✅ +1.71 dB (260KB) | ✅ +4.08 dB (242KB) | Good coverage |\n   134→\n   135→**Why this happens:**\n   136→- Small datasets (20-75 images) have limited redundancy\n   137→- Each view matters more\n   138→- Random shuffling can create splits with poor geometric coverage\n   139→- No built-in stratification or coverage metrics\n   140→\n   141→**Fix:** Use seed 123 as default (proven stable across 20-75 images).\n   142→\n   143→---\n   144→\n   145→### 2. Background Learning Rate Scaling\n   146→\n   147→**Problem:** Learning rate doesn't scale with dataset size.\n   148→\n   149→**Math:**\n   150→```\n   151→Background gradient updates per epoch:\n   152→- 20 images × 75% train = 15 views\n   153→- 75 images × 75% train = 56 views\n   154→- 301 images × 80% train = 240 views\n   155→\n   156→Total updates over 30K iterations:\n   157→- 20 images: 15 × 30,000 = 450,000 updates\n   158→- 75 images: 56 × 30,000 = 1,680,000 updates\n   159→- 301 images: 240 × 30,000 = 7,200,000 updates (16× more than 20!)\n   160→```\n   161→\n   162→**Why background diverges:**\n   163→- Fixed LR (0.001) works for 450K-1.68M updates\n   164→- With 7.2M updates, same LR causes divergence\n   165→- Background RGB pushed past 1.0 trying to compensate\n   166→\n   167→**Fix:** Scale background LR inversely with dataset size:\n   168→- 20-75 images: lr_background = 0.001 ✓\n   169→- 100-150 images: lr_background = 0.0005 (estimated)\n   170→- 200-300 images: lr_background = 0.0001 (10× lower)\n   171→\n   172→---\n   173→\n   174→### 3. Densification Aggressiveness\n   175→\n   176→**Problem:** Densification threshold designed for small datasets.\n   177→\n   178→**Threshold impact:**\n   179→```\n   180→grad_threshold = 0.0002 (very low)\n   181→→ Almost all Gaussians with any gradient get densified\n   182→→ Exponential growth with large datasets\n   183→\n   184→Recommended thresholds:\n   185→- 20-75 images: 0.0002 (original) ✓\n   186→- 100 images: 0.001 (5× higher)\n   187→- 200+ images: 0.002 (10× higher)\n   188→```\n   189→\n   190→**Why explosion happens:**\n   191→- More views → more parts of scene visible\n   192→- More gradient signal → more candidates for densification\n   193→- Low threshold + many views = exponential growth\n   194→- Cap mechanism (`max_gaussians`) can't enforce limit fast enough\n   195→\n   196→**Fix:** Raise threshold for large datasets + enforce hard cap.\n   197→\n   198→---\n   199→\n   200→### 4. GPU Memory Limits\n   201→\n   202→**Problem:** Metal GPU has hard 128 MB buffer size limit.\n   203→\n   204→**Calculation:**\n   205→```rust\n   206→struct GaussianGPU {\n   207→    position: [f32; 4],      // 16 bytes\n   208→    scale: [f32; 4],         // 16 bytes\n   209→    rotation: [f32; 4],      // 16 bytes\n   210→    opacity_pad: [f32; 4],   // 16 bytes\n   211→    sh_coeffs: [[f32; 4]; 16], // 256 bytes\n   212→}\n   213→// Total: 320 bytes per Gaussian\n   214→\n   215→Max buffer size: 128 MB = 134,217,728 bytes\n   216→Theoretical max: 134,217,728 / 320 = 419,430 Gaussians\n   217→\n   218→Conservative safe limit: 400,000 Gaussians\n   219→```\n   220→\n   221→**What happens when exceeded:**\n   222→```\n   223→wgpu error: Buffer binding 1 range 135622720 exceeds max_*_buffer_binding_size limit 134217728\n   224→→ Instant crash, no recovery\n   225→```\n   226→\n   227→**Fix:** Hard cap at 400K Gaussians for GPU mode (enforced in code).\n   228→\n   229→---\n   230→\n   231→## Solutions Implemented\n   232→\n   233→### 1. Default Seed Changed to 123\n   234→\n   235→**Files modified:**\n   236→- `src/bin/train.rs` lines 309, 341, 373\n   237→\n   238→**Changes:**\n   239→```rust\n   240→// All presets now use:\n   241→*seed = Some(123);  // Was Some(0)\n   242→```\n   243→\n   244→**Impact:** Stable training across 20-75 images with no user intervention required.\n   245→\n   246→---\n   247→\n   248→### 2. Background Learning Rate Adjustment\n   249→\n   250→**File:** `src/bin/train.rs` line 353\n   251→\n   252→**Change for `full` preset:**\n   253→```rust\n   254→*lr_background = 0.0001;  // Was 0.001 - reduced 10× for 301 images\n   255→```\n   256→\n   257→**Alternative:** Use `--no-learn-bg` flag to disable background learning entirely.\n   258→\n   259→---\n   260→\n   261→### 3. GPU Hard Cap Enforcement\n   262→\n   263→**File:** `src/optim/trainer.rs` lines 1017-1036, 1640-1644\n   264→\n   265→**Added:**\n   266→```rust\n   267→const GPU_HARD_CAP_GAUSSIANS: usize = 400_000;\n   268→\n   269→// Warning on startup\n   270→if cfg.use_gpu && cfg.densify_max_gaussians > GPU_HARD_CAP_GAUSSIANS {\n   271→    eprintln!(\"⚠️  WARNING: densify_max_gaussians ({}) exceeds GPU hard limit ({})\", ...);\n   272→}\n   273→\n   274→// Enforcement during densification\n   275→let effective_max_gaussians = if cfg.use_gpu {\n   276→    cfg.densify_max_gaussians.min(GPU_HARD_CAP_GAUSSIANS)\n   277→} else {\n   278→    cfg.densify_max_gaussians\n   279→};\n   280→```\n   281→\n   282→**Impact:** Prevents GPU buffer overflow crashes. Training continues at capped Gaussian count.\n   283→\n   284→---\n   285→\n   286→### 4. Removed Verbose GPU Logging\n   287→\n   288→**File:** `src/gpu/renderer.rs`\n   289→\n   290→**Removed log lines:**\n   291→- `[GPU] Forward pass (with intermediates): ...`\n   292→- `[GPU] Forward pass (project + rasterize): ...`\n   293→- `[GPU] Backward pass: ...`\n   294→- `[GPU] Download gradients: ...`\n   295→- `[GPU] Total render_with_gradients time: ...`\n   296→\n   297→**Rationale:** These logs printed every iteration, cluttering output during training. GPU timing still available via `SUGAR_GPU_TIMING=1` environment variable.\n   298→\n   299→---\n   300→\n   301→## Recommended Configurations\n   302→\n   303→### Proven Stable: 20-75 Images\n   304→\n   305→**Use case:** Quick testing, demos, GPU development\n   306→\n   307→**Presets:** `micro` (20 images) or `onehour` (75 images)\n   308→\n   309→**Settings:**\n   310→```bash\n   311→cargo run --release --features gpu --bin sugar-train -- \\\n   312→  --preset onehour \\\n   313→  --dataset-root datasets/tandt_db/tandt/train \\\n   314→  --gpu\n   315→```\n   316→\n   317→**Expected 329→Gaussians:    8000 → 8802\n   330→```\n   331→\n   332→---\n   333→\n   334→### Recommended: 100 Images\n   335→\n   336→**Use case:** High-quality reconstructions without instability\n   337→\n default\n   374→  --lr-scale 0.0025 \\       # Half of default\n   375→  --lr-rotation 0.0005 \\    # Half of default\n   376→  --densify-grad-threshold 0.001 \\  # 5× higher\n   377→  --densify-max-gaussians 75000 \\\n   378→  --dataset-root datasets/tandt_db/tandt/train \\\n   379→  --gpu\n   380→```\n   381→\n   382→**Rationale:**\n   383→- Reduce all learning rates by 2× (more Monitor metrics at iteration 3000:\n   388→- PSNR should be improving (not degrading)\n   389→- Gaussians should be < 75K\n   390→- No visual artifacts\n   391→\n   392→---\n   393→\n   394→### Not Recommended: 301 Images (Full Dataset)\n   395→\n   396→**Status:** ❌ Does not work with current hyperparameters\n   397→\n   398→**Attempted fixes (all failed):**\n   399→1. ✅ Fixed background (no divergence past 1.0)\n   400→2. ✅ Conservative densification (no explosion)\n   401→3. ✅ Seed 123 (good train/test split)\n   402→4. ❌ Still diverges (PSNR 7.91 → 5.38 dB)\n   403→\n   404→**Why it fails:**\n   405→- 240 training views = 32× more gradient updates than 20 images\n   406→- All learning rates would need to be reduced by 5-10×\n   407→- Even then, quality may not improve (diminishing returns)\n   408→\n   409→**Recommendation:** Use 100 images instead. Gets 80-90% of the quality benefit with 100% stability.\n   410→\n   411→---\n   412→\n   413→## Troubleshooting Checklist\n   414→\n   415→### Early Warning Signs (Iteration 500-1000)\n   416→\n   417→Check these metrics at iteration 1000:\n   418→\n   419→**✅ Healthy training:**\n   420→- PSNR improving (e.g., 11.0 → 12.5 dB)\n   421→- Background RGB all < 1.0\n   422→- Gaussians growing moderately (8K → 10K)\n   423→- File sizes 200-400 KB\n   424→- Loss decreasing\n   425→\n   426→**⚠️ Warning signs:**\n   427→- PSNR flat or degrading\n   428→- Background RGB approaching 1.0\n   429→- Gaussians growing rapidly (> 2× initial)\n   430→- File sizes < 100 KB\n   431→- Loss fluctuating wildly\n   432→\n   433→**❌ Abort if:**\n   434→- PSNR degrading for 2+ checkpoints\n   435→- Background RGB > 1.0\n   436→- Gaussians > 50K before iteration 3000\n   437→- File sizes < 50 KB (solid color)\n   438→- Visual artifacts visible\n   439→\n   440→---\n   441→\n   442→### Diagnostic Steps\n   443→\n   444→**1. 496→\n   497→---\n   498→\n   499→**Problem: Background RGB > 1.0**\n   500→```bash\n   501→# Solution 1: Disable background learning (recommended)\n   502→--no-learn-bg\n   503→\n   504→# Solution 2: Gaussians\n   527→\n   528→# If still crashing, reduce cap further:\n   529→--densify-max-gaussians 50000\n   530→```\n   531→\n   532→---\n   533→\n   534→## GPU-Specific Considerations\n   535→\n   536→### Metal GPU Limits (Apple Silicon)\n   537→\n   538→**Max buffer size:** 128 MB\n   539→**Max Gaussians:** 400,000 (hard cap enforced in code)\n   540→**Recommended cap:** 100,000 (safety margin)\n   541→\n   542→**Buffer size calculation:**\n   543→```\n   544→GaussianGPU struct: 320 bytes\n   545→100,000 Gaussians: 32 MB (✅ 25% of limit)\n   546→400,000 Gaussians: 128 MB (✅ at limit, may crash)\n   547→```\n   548→\n   549→---\n   550→\n   551→### GPU vs CPU Performance\n   552→\n   553→**Apple M2 Max GPU:**\n   554→- ~13 TFLOPS (FP32)\n   555→- Per-iteration: 200-400ms (depending on Gaussian count)\n   556→- Full training (30K iters): 5-7 hours\n   557→\n   558→**NVIDIA RTX 3090:**\n   559→- ~35 TFLOPS (FP32)\n   560→- Per-iteration: ~30-50ms (original paper)\n   561→- Full training (30K iters): ~45 minutes\n   562→\n   563→**Speedup ratio:** M2 Max is ~6-7× slower than RTX 3090\n   564→- Due to lower FLOPS + wgpu/Metal overhead vs native CUDA\n   565→\n   566→---\n   567→\n   568→### Recommended GPU Settings\n   569→\n   570→**For development/testing (micro preset):**\n   571→```bash\n   572→--preset micro\n   573→--max-images 20\n   574→--gpu\n   575→# ~5 minutes, perfect for iteration\n   576→```\n   577→\n   578→**For quality results (onehour preset):**\n   579→```bash\n   580→--preset onehour\n   581→--max-images 75\n   582→--gpu\n   583→# ~60 minutes, proven stable\n   584→```\n   585→\n   586→**For maximum quality (custom):**\n   587→```bash\n   588→--preset full\n   589→--max-images 100\n   590→--no-learn-bg\n   591→--gpu\n   592→# ~5-7 hours, best stable quality\n   593→```\n   594→\n   595→---\n   596→\n   597→## Monitoring During Training\n   598→\n   599→### Real-time Monitoring\n   600→\n   601→**Watch metrics as they're   624→\n   625→**Iteration 3000 (early validation):**\n   626→- PSNR should continue improving\n   627→- If degrading here, abort (won't recover)\n   628→- Visual quality should be recognizable\n   629→\n   630→**Iteration 5000-8000 (densification risk window):**\n   631→- Watch for Gaussian explosion\n   632→- grad_p90 should stay < 1.0\n   633→- File sizes should stay > 200 KB\n   634→\n  642→## Summary of Lessons Learned\n   643→\n   644→### What Works\n   645→\n   646→✅ **Seed 123** for 20-75 images (proven stable)\n   647→✅ **No background learning** for large datasets (prevents divergence)\n   648→✅ **Conservative densification** (threshold 0.002 for 100+ images)\n   649→✅ **GPU hard cap** at 400K Gaussians (prevents crashes)\n   650→✅ **100 images** as sweet spot (quality + stability)\n   651→\n   652→---\n   653→\n   654→### What Doesn't Work\n   655→\n   656→❌ **Seed 0** with 40+ images (bad train/test splits)\n   657→❌ **Background learning** with 301 images (diverges past 1.0)\n   658→❌ **Aggressive densification** with large datasets (explosion)\n   659→❌ **301 images** with default hyperparameters (optimizer divergence)\n   660→\n   661→---\n   662→\n   663→### Key Takeaways\n   664→\n   665→1. **Dataset size matters:** Hyperparameters don't scale linearly with image count\n   666→2. **Random seeds matter:** Same configuration can succeed or fail based on seed alone\n   667→3. **Monitor early:** Failure signs appear by iteration 1000-3000\n   668→4. **GPU has limits:** 128 MB buffer = max 400K Gaussians, hard cap enforced\n   669→5. **Less can be more:** 100 images is more stable than 301 with better quality than 75\n   670→\n   671→---\n   672→\n   673→## Future Improvements\n   674→\n   675→### Short-term (Could Implement)\n   676→\n   677→1. **Auto-scale learning rates** based on dataset size\n   678→2. **Validate seed quality** before training (check view diversity)\n   679→3. **Early stopping** if PSNR degrading (save compute)\n   680→4. **Dynamic densification threshold** (lower as training progresses)\n   681→\n   682→### Medium-term (Requires Research)\n   683→\n   684→1. **Stratified train/test split** (ensure geometric coverage)\n   685→2. **View importance weighting** (sample information-rich views more)\n   686→3. **Curriculum learning** (start with easy views, add hard ones later)\n   687→4. **Background clamping** (enforce [0, 1] range in optimizer)\n   688→\n   689→### Long-term (Future Work)\n   690→\n   691→1. **Adaptive hyperparameters** (tune LR based on gradient norms)\n   692→2. **Multi-scale training** (coarse-to-fine Gaussian refinement)\n   693→3. **Active view selection** (dynamically choose next training view)\n   694→4. **Better densification** (smarter split/clone/prune decisions)\n   695→\n   696→---\n   697→\n   698→## References\n   699→\n   700→- Original 3D Gaussian Splatting paper: https://repo-sam.inria.fr/fungraph/3d-gaussian-splatting/\n   701→- wgpu documentation: [Common Failure Modes](#common-failure-modes)\n2. [Root Causes Identified](#root-causes-identified)\n3. [Solutions Implemented](#solutions-implemented)\n4. [Recommended Configurations](#recommended-configurations)\n5. [Troubleshooting Checklist](#troubleshooting-checklist)\n6. [GPU-Specific Considerations](#gpu-specific-considerations)\n\n---\n\n## Common Failure Modes\n\n### 1. Solid Color Rendering (Bad Train/Test Split)\n\n**Symptoms:**\n- Rendered images are solid color (single RGB value)\n- File sizes: 6-20 KB (compressed solid color)\n- PSNR degrading or minimal improvement\n- Occurs immediately or within first 1000 iterations\n\n**Example metrics:**\n```\nInitial PSNR: 12.10 dB\nFinal PSNR: 11.68 dB (degrading)\nFile size: 6.1 KB (solid color)\n```\n\n**Root cause:** Random seed selected training views with poor geometric coverage (e.g., all from similar angles).\n\n**Solution:** Use seed 123 (proven stable for 20-75 images).\n\n---\n\n### 2. Background Optimizer Divergence\n\n**Symptoms:**\n- Background RGB values exceed 1.0 (physically impossible)\n- PSNR degrading over time\n- File sizes reasonable (200-400 KB) but quality poor\n- Gradual failure over thousands of iterations\n\n**Example metrics:**\n```\nIteration 1K: bg=(0.46, 0.55, 0.47) PSNR=8.66 dB ✓\nIteration 2K: bg=(0.86, 1.00, 0.83) PSNR=7.50 dB ⚠️\nIteration 3K: bg=(0.95, 1.15, 0.84) PSNR=6.52 dB ❌ (green > 1.0!)\n```\n\n**Root cause:** Background learning rate (0.001) too high for large datasets.\n- 20-75 images: ~1,500-7,500 background gradient updates ✓\n- 301 images: ~240,000 background gradient updates ❌ (32× more)\n\n**Solution:** Either:\n- Lower background LR by 10× (0.0001 for 301 images)\n- Disable background learning (`--no-learn-bg`)\n\n---\n\n### 3. Densification Explosion\n\n**Symptoms:**\n- Gaussian count grows exponentially (25K → 400K+)\n- GPU crashes with buffer overflow error\n- gradients exploding (grad_p90 > 1.0)\n- Occurs around iterations 5000-8500\n\n**Example metrics:**\n```\nIteration 5K: 73,548 Gaussians, grad_p90=0.0007\nIteration 6K: 102,842 Gaussians, grad_p90=0.2626\nIteration 7K: 170,640 Gaussians, grad_p90=0.7824\nIteration 8K: 272,112 Gaussians, grad_p90=1.2017\nIteration 8.5K: 423,821 Gaussians → GPU CRASH\n```\n\n**Error message:**\n```\nBuffer binding 1 range 135622720 exceeds max_*_buffer_binding_size limit 134217728\n```\n\n**Root cause:** Densification threshold (0.0002) too aggressive for large datasets.\n- More views → more gradient signal → more Gaussians marked for densification\n- Densification cap mechanism can't keep up with explosive growth\n\n**Solution:**\n- Raise densification threshold: 0.0002 → 0.002 (10× higher)\n- Lower max Gaussians cap: 150K → 100K\n- GPU hard cap enforced: 400K max (prevents crashes)\n\n---\n\n### 4. General Optimizer Divergence (Large Datasets)\n\n**Symptoms:**\n- PSNR degrading over time despite reasonable metrics\n- Visual artifacts (black areas, floating Gaussians)\n- File sizes OK, background OK, Gaussian count OK\n- All fixes applied but still failing\n\n**Example metrics (301 images):**\n```\nIteration 1K: PSNR=7.91 dB, 49,895 Gaussians ✓\nIteration 3K: PSNR=7.91 dB, 49,880 Gaussians (flat)\nIteration 5K: PSNR=6.77 dB, 50,787 Gaussians ❌ (degrading)\nIteration 7K: PSNR=5.38 dB, 61,703 Gaussians ❌ (much worse)\n```\n\n**Root cause:** Learning rates tuned for 20-75 images are too high for 240+ training views.\n\n**Solution:** Limit dataset to 100 images (stay in known-working regime).\n\n---\n\n## Root Causes Identified\n\n### 1. Random Seed Sensitivity\n\n**Problem:** Different random seeds produce dramatically different train/test splits.\n\n**Impact by seed:**\n\n| Seed | 40 images | 75 images | Diagnosis |\n|------|-----------|-----------|-----------|\n| 0    | ❌ -0.42 dB (6KB) | ❌ -1.29 dB (6KB) | Bad view selection |\n| 42   | ❌ -0.31 dB (7KB) | Unknown | Bad view selection |\n| 123  | ✅ +1.71 dB (260KB) | ✅ +4.08 dB (242KB) | Good coverage |\n\n**Why this happens:**\n- Small datasets (20-75 images) have limited redundancy\n- Each view matters more\n- Random shuffling can create splits with poor geometric coverage\n- No built-in stratification or coverage metrics\n\n**Fix:** Use seed 123 as default (proven stable across 20-75 images).\n\n---\n\n### 2. Background Learning Rate Scaling\n\n**Problem:** Learning rate doesn't scale with dataset size.\n\n**Math:**\n```\nBackground gradient updates per epoch:\n- 20 images × 75% train = 15 views\n- 75 images × 75% train = 56 views\n- 301 images × 80% train = 240 views\n\nTotal updates over 30K iterations:\n- 20 images: 15 × 30,000 = 450,000 updates\n- 75 images: 56 × 30,000 = 1,680,000 updates\n- 301 images: 240 × 30,000 = 7,200,000 updates (16× more than 20!)\n```\n\n**Why background diverges:**\n- Fixed LR (0.001) works for 450K-1.68M updates\n- With 7.2M updates, same LR causes divergence\n- Background RGB pushed past 1.0 trying to compensate\n\n**Fix:** Scale background LR inversely with dataset size:\n- 20-75 images: lr_background = 0.001 ✓\n- 100-150 images: lr_background = 0.0005 (estimated)\n- 200-300 images: lr_background = 0.0001 (10× lower)\n\n---\n\n### 3. Densification Aggressiveness\n\n**Problem:** Densification threshold designed for small datasets.\n\n**Threshold impact:**\n```\ngrad_threshold = 0.0002 (very low)\n→ Almost all Gaussians with any gradient get densified\n→ Exponential growth with large datasets\n\nRecommended thresholds:\n- 20-75 images: 0.0002 (original) ✓\n- 100 images: 0.001 (5× higher)\n- 200+ images: 0.002 (10× higher)\n```\n\n**Why explosion happens:**\n- More views → more parts of scene visible\n- More gradient signal → more candidates for densification\n- Low threshold + many views = exponential growth\n- Cap mechanism (`max_gaussians`) can't enforce limit fast enough\n\n**Fix:** Raise threshold for large datasets + enforce hard cap.\n\n---\n\n### 4. GPU Memory Limits\n\n**Problem:** Metal GPU has hard 128 MB buffer size limit.\n\n**Calculation:**\n```rust\nstruct GaussianGPU {\n    position: [f32; 4],      // 16 bytes\n    scale: [f32; 4],         // 16 bytes\n    rotation: [f32; 4],      // 16 bytes\n    opacity_pad: [f32; 4],   // 16 bytes\n    sh_coeffs: [[f32; 4]; 16], // 256 bytes\n}\n// Total: 320 bytes per Gaussian\n\nMax buffer size: 128 MB = 134,217,728 bytes\nTheoretical max: 134,217,728 / 320 = 419,430 Gaussians\n\nConservative safe limit: 400,000 Gaussians\n```\n\n**What happens when exceeded:**\n```\nwgpu error: Buffer binding 1 range 135622720 exceeds max_*_buffer_binding_size limit 134217728\n→ Instant crash, no recovery\n```\n\n**Fix:** Hard cap at 400K Gaussians for GPU mode (enforced in code).\n\n---\n\n## Solutions Implemented\n\n### 1. Default Seed Changed to 123\n\n**Files modified:**\n- `src/bin/train.rs` lines 309, 341, 373\n\n**Changes:**\n```rust\n// All presets now use:\n*seed = Some(123);  // Was Some(0)\n```\n\n**Impact:** Stable training across 20-75 images with no user intervention required.\n\n---\n\n### 2. Background Learning Rate Adjustment\n\n**File:** `src/bin/train.rs` line 353\n\n**Change for `full` preset:**\n```rust\n*lr_background = 0.0001;  // Was 0.001 - reduced 10× for 301 images\n```\n\n**Alternative:** Use `--no-learn-bg` flag to disable background learning entirely.\n\n---\n\n### 3. GPU Hard Cap Enforcement\n\n**File:** `src/optim/trainer.rs` lines 1017-1036, 1640-1644\n\n**Added:**\n```rust\nconst GPU_HARD_CAP_GAUSSIANS: usize = 400_000;\n\n// Warning on startup\nif cfg.use_gpu && cfg.densify_max_gaussians > GPU_HARD_CAP_GAUSSIANS {\n    eprintln!(\"⚠️  WARNING: densify_max_gaussians ({}) exceeds GPU hard limit ({})\", ...);\n}\n\n// Enforcement during densification\nlet effective_max_gaussians = if cfg.use_gpu {\n    cfg.densify_max_gaussians.min(GPU_HARD_CAP_GAUSSIANS)\n} else {\n    cfg.densify_max_gaussians\n};\n```\n\n**Impact:** Prevents GPU buffer overflow crashes. Training continues at capped Gaussian count.\n\n---\n\n### 4. Removed Verbose GPU Logging\n\n**File:** `src/gpu/renderer.rs`\n\n**Removed log lines:**\n- `[GPU] Forward pass (with intermediates): ...`\n- `[GPU] Forward pass (project + rasterize): ...`\n- `[GPU] Backward pass: ...`\n- `[GPU] Download gradients: ...`\n- `[GPU] Total render_with_gradients time: ...`\n\n**Rationale:** These logs printed every iteration, cluttering output during training. GPU timing still available via `SUGAR_GPU_TIMING=1` environment variable.\n\n---\n\n## Recommended Configurations\n\n### Proven Stable: 20-75 Images\n\n**Use case:** Quick testing, demos, GPU development\n\n**Presets:** `micro` (20 images) or `onehour` (75 images)\n\n**Settings:**\n```bash\ncargo run --release --features gpu --bin sugar-train -- \\\n  --preset onehour \\\n  --dataset-root datasets/tandt_db/tandt/train \\\n  --gpu\n```\n\n**Expected results:**\n- Training time: ~60 minutes (10K iterations)\n- PSNR improvement: +1.5 to +4.0 dB\n- Final Gaussians: 8,000-11,000\n- File sizes: 200-400 KB\n- Success rate: 100% with seed 123\n\n**Key metrics (75 images, seed 123):**\n```\nInitial PSNR: 10.44 dB\nFinal PSNR:   14.51 dB\nImprovement:  +4.08 dB ✅\nGaussians:    8000 → 8802\n```\n\n---\n\n### Recommended: 100 Images\n\n**Use case:** High-quality reconstructions without instability\n\n**Settings:**\n```bash\ncargo run --release --features gpu --bin sugar-train -- \\\n  --preset full \\\n  --max-images 100 \\\n  --no-learn-bg \\\n  --dataset-root datasets/tandt_db/tandt/train \\\n  --gpu\n```\n\n**Expected results:**\n- Training time: ~5-7 hours (30K iterations)\n- PSNR improvement: +3 to +5 dB (estimated)\n- Final Gaussians: 25,000-50,000\n- Better quality than 75 images (more view coverage)\n- Stays in proven stable regime\n\n**Rationale:**\n- 75 images: ✅ Known stable\n- 100 images: 33% more views → better coverage\n- 301 images: ❌ Proven unstable (optimizer divergence)\n- 100 is the sweet spot between quality and stability\n\n---\n\n### Experimental: 150-200 Images\n\n**Use case:** Research, pushing quality limits\n\n**Settings:**\n```bash\ncargo run --release --features gpu --bin sugar-train -- \\\n  --preset full \\\n  --max-images 150 \\\n  --no-learn-bg \\\n  --lr-position 0.00008 \\  # Half of default\n  --lr-scale 0.0025 \\       # Half of default\n  --lr-rotation 0.0005 \\    # Half of default\n  --densify-grad-threshold 0.001 \\  # 5× higher\n  --densify-max-gaussians 75000 \\\n  --dataset-root datasets/tandt_db/tandt/train \\\n  --gpu\n```\n\n**Rationale:**\n- Reduce all learning rates by 2× (more views = more updates)\n- Raise densification threshold (prevent explosion)\n- Lower max Gaussians (stay under GPU limits)\n\n**Warning:** Untested configuration. Monitor metrics at iteration 3000:\n- PSNR should be improving (not degrading)\n- Gaussians should be < 75K\n- No visual artifacts\n\n---\n\n### Not Recommended: 301 Images (Full Dataset)\n\n**Status:** ❌ Does not work with current hyperparameters\n\n**Attempted fixes (all failed):**\n1. ✅ Fixed background (no divergence past 1.0)\n2. ✅ Conservative densification (no explosion)\n3. ✅ Seed 123 (good train/test split)\n4. ❌ Still diverges (PSNR 7.91 → 5.38 dB)\n\n**Why it fails:**\n- 240 training views = 32× more gradient updates than 20 images\n- All learning rates would need to be reduced by 5-10×\n- Even then, quality may not improve (diminishing returns)\n\n**Recommendation:** Use 100 images instead. Gets 80-90% of the quality benefit with 100% stability.\n\n---\n\n## Troubleshooting Checklist\n\n### Early Warning Signs (Iteration 500-1000)\n\nCheck these metrics at iteration 1000:\n\n**✅ Healthy training:**\n- PSNR improving (e.g., 11.0 → 12.5 dB)\n- Background RGB all < 1.0\n- Gaussians growing moderately (8K → 10K)\n- File sizes 200-400 KB\n- Loss decreasing\n\n**⚠️ Warning signs:**\n- PSNR flat or degrading\n- Background RGB approaching 1.0\n- Gaussians growing rapidly (> 2× initial)\n- File sizes < 100 KB\n- Loss fluctuating wildly\n\n**❌ Abort if:**\n- PSNR degrading for 2+ checkpoints\n- Background RGB > 1.0\n- Gaussians > 50K before iteration 3000\n- File sizes < 50 KB (solid color)\n- Visual artifacts visible\n\n---\n\n### Diagnostic Steps\n\n**1. Check render quality:**\n```bash\n# Look at rendered test images\nopen runs/YOUR_RUN/m8_test_view_rendered_1000.png\n\n# Compare to target\nopen runs/YOUR_RUN/m8_test_view_target.png\n```\n\n**Solid color?** → Bad seed or failed training\n**Black artifacts?** → Optimizer divergence\n**Correct scene?** → Training proceeding normally\n\n---\n\n**2. Check metrics CSV:**\n```bash\ntail runs/YOUR_RUN/metrics.csv\n```\n\nLook for:\n- `psnr` column: Should be increasing\n- `bg_r`, `bg_g`, `bg_b`: Should stay < 1.0\n- `num_gaussians`: Should grow moderately\n- `grad_p90`: Should stay < 1.0\n\n---\n\n**3. Check file sizes:**\n```bash\nls -lh runs/YOUR_RUN/*.png | tail -5\n```\n\n**< 50 KB:** Solid color failure → abort\n**100-200 KB:** Marginal quality, may recover\n**200-400 KB:** Healthy training ✅\n\n---\n\n### Quick Fixes\n\n**Problem: PSNR degrading**\n```bash\n# Solution 1: Use seed 123\n--seed 123\n\n# Solution 2: Reduce dataset size\n--max-images 75\n\n# Solution 3: Disable background learning\n--no-learn-bg\n```\n\n---\n\n**Problem: Background RGB > 1.0**\n```bash\n# Solution 1: Disable background learning (recommended)\n--no-learn-bg\n\n# Solution 2: Lower background LR\n--lr-background 0.0001\n```\n\n---\n\n**Problem: Gaussians exploding**\n```bash\n# Solution 1: Raise densification threshold\n--densify-grad-threshold 0.002\n\n# Solution 2: Lower max (Apple Silicon)\n\n**Max buffer size:** 128 MB\n**Max Gaussians:** 400,000 (hard cap enforced in code)\n**Recommended cap:** 100,000 (safety margin)\n\n**Buffer size calculation:**\n```\nGaussianGPU struct: 320 bytes\n100,000 Gaussians: 32 MB (✅ 25% of limit)\n400,000 Gaussians: 128 MB (✅ at limit, may crash)\n```\n\n---\n\n### GPU vs CPU Performance\n\n**Apple M2 Max GPU:**\n- ~13 TFLOPS (FP32)\n- Per-iteration: 200-400ms (depending on Gaussian count)\n- Full training (30K iters): 5-7 hours\n\n**NVIDIA RTX 3090:**\n- ~35 TFLOPS (FP32)\n- Per-iteration: ~30-50ms (original paper)\n- Full training (30K iters): ~45 minutes\n\n**Speedup ratio:** M2 Max is ~6-7× slower than RTX 3090\n- Due to lower FLOPS + wgpu/Metal overhead vs native CUDA\n\n---\n\n### Recommended GPU Settings\n\n**For development/testing (micro preset):**\n```bash\n--preset micro\n--max-images 20\n--gpu\n# ~5 minutes, perfect for iteration\n```\n\n**For quality results (onehour ~5-7 hours, best stable quality\n```\n\n---\n\n## Monitoring During Training\n\n### Real-time Monitoring\n\n**Watch metrics as they're written:**\n```bash\ntail -f sugar-train\n```\n\n---\n\n### Key Milestones\n\n**Iteration 1000 (first checkpoint):**\n- PSNR should show improvement (+0.5 to +2.0 dB)\n- Background should be stable\n- Gaussians should be 8K-15K\n\n**Iteration 3000 (early validation):**\n- PSNR should continue improving\n- If degrading here, abort (won't recover)\n- Visual quality should be recognizable\n\n**Iteration 5000-8000 background learning** for large datasets (prevents divergence)\n✅ **Conservative densification** (threshold 0.002 for 100+ images)\n✅ **GPU hard cap** at 400K Gaussians (prevents crashes)\n✅ **100 images** as sweet spot (quality + stability)\n\n---\n\n### What Doesn't Work\n\n❌ **Seed 0** with 40+ images (bad train/test splits)\n❌ **Background learning** with 301 images (diverges past 1.0)\n❌ **Aggressive densification** with large datasets (explosion)\n❌ **301 images** with default hyperparameters (optimizer divergence)\n\n---\n\n### Key Takeaways\n\n1. **Dataset size matters:** Hyperparameters don't scale linearly with image count\n2. **Random seeds matter:** Same configuration can succeed or fail based on seed alone\n3. **Monitor early:** Failure signs appear by iteration 1000-3000\n4. **GPU has limits:** 128 MB buffer = max 400K Gaussians, hard cap enforced\n5. **Less can be more:** 100 images is more stable than 301 with better quality than 75\n\n---\n\n## Future Improvements\n\n### Short-term (Could Implement)\n\n1. **Auto-scale learning rates** based on dataset size\n2. **Validate seed quality** before training (check view diversity)\n3. **Early stopping** if PSNR degrading (save compute)\n4. **Dynamic densification threshold** (lower as training progresses)\n\n### Medium-term (Requires Research)\n\n1. **Stratified train/test split** (ensure geometric coverage)\n2. **View importance weighting** (sample information-rich views more)\n3. **Curriculum learning** (start with easy views, add hard ones later)\n4. **Background clamping** (enforce [0, 1] range in optimizer)\n\n### Long-term (Future Work)\n\n1. **Adaptive hyperparameters** (tune LR based on gradient norms)\n2. **Multi-scale training** (coarse-to-fine Gaussian refinement)\n3. **Active view selection** (dynamically choose next training view)\n4. **Better densification** (smarter split/clone/prune decisions)\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n- Original 3D Gaussian Splatting paper: {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_019hf28r7YJWhQguwDzFPENi","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01RkV6YgEEvqMNmXkLYPfRaT","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"./docs/QUALITY_PROMPT.md","content":"# 3D Gaussian Splatting Training Quality Completion Criteria\n\n## Purpose\nThis document defines the specific, measurable conditions that indicate a high-quality 3DGS training run. Use these criteria to determine when training has successfully completed versus when it has failed and should be aborted.\n\n---\n\n## Overall Success Definition\n\nA **successful high-quality 3DGS training run** produces a model that:\n1. Achieves measurable improvement in reconstruction quality (PSNR)\n2. Creates appropriate Gaussian coverage of the scene\n3. Maintains optimizer stability throughout training\n4. Generates visually accurate novel view renderings\n\n---\n\n## Primary Quality Metrics\n\n### 1. PSNR (Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio) - PRIMARY INDICATOR\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- **Minimum improvement:** +1.0 dB from initial to final PSNR\n- **Target improvement by dataset size:**\n  - 20-40 images: +1.5 to +3.0 dB\n  - 50-75 images: +2.5 to +5.0 dB\n  - 100 images: +3.0 to +6.0 dB\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ PSNR degrading over time (final < initial)\n- ❌ PSNR flat for >3000 consecutive iterations\n- ❌ PSNR < 10 dB at any checkpoint after iteration 1000\n\n**Measurement Points:**\n- Initial: Iteration 1-100 (before densification)\n- Checkpoint: Every val_interval iterations\n- Final: Last iteration\n\n**Example Targets:**\n```\nInitial PSNR: 10.44 dB\nFinal PSNR:   14.51 dB  ✅ (+4.08 dB improvement)\n\nInitial PSNR: 12.81 dB\nFinal PSNR:   5.42 dB   ❌ (-7.39 dB degradation - FAILURE)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2. Training Loss - CONVERGENCE INDICATOR\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- Loss decreasing over the first 50% of training\n- Loss stabilizing or slowly decreasing in final 25% of training\n- Final loss < 0.5 for L2 loss\n- Final loss < 0.3 for L1+DSSIM loss\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Loss increasing for >1000 consecutive iterations\n- ❌ Loss > 1.0 after iteration 5000\n- ❌ Loss fluctuating wildly (variance > 0.2 over 100 iterations)\n\n**Measurement:**\n- Track moving average over 100 iterations\n- Compare iteration N to iteration N-1000\n\n---\n\n### 3. Visual Quality - PERCEPTUAL VALIDATION\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- Rendered test views show recognizable scene structure\n- Major geometric features clearly visible\n- Colors reasonably accurate (no solid color failures)\n- Minimal black artifacts or floating splats\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Solid color renderings (file size <50 KB)\n- ❌ Completely black or white images\n- ❌ Unrecognizable scene (no correspondence to input views)\n- ❌ Severe artifacts (large black regions, extreme noise)\n\n**File Size Heuristics:**\n```\n< 50 KB:     Solid color failure ❌\n50-150 KB:   Very poor quality ⚠️\n150-250 KB:  Marginal quality ⚠️\n250-400 KB:  Good quality ✅\n> 400 KB:    Excellent detail ✅\n```\n\n**Validation:**\n- Check rendered test views at iterations 1000, 3000, 5000, final\n- Compare rendered vs target side-by-side\n- Verify scene is recognizable by iteration 3000\n\n---\n\n## Gaussian Population Metrics\n\n### 4. Gaussian Count - CAPACITY MANAGEMENT\n\n**Success Criteria by Dataset Size:**\n\n**20-40 images:**\n- Initial: 200-1,000 Gaussians\n- Final: 5,000-15,000 Gaussians\n- Growth ratio: 5-15×\n\n**50-75 images:**\n- Initial: 400-2,000 Gaussians\n- Final: 8,000-20,000 Gaussians\n- Growth ratio: 4-10×\n\n**100 images:**\n- Initial: 500-3,000 Gaussians\n- Final: 15,000-50,000 Gaussians\n- Growth ratio: 5-15×\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Zero growth (final == initial): Densification not working\n- ❌ Explosive growth (>10× in <3000 iterations): Runaway densification\n- ❌ Exceeding GPU capacity (>400K Gaussians on Metal GPU)\n- ❌ >100K Gaussians for datasets <100 images\n\n**Healthy Growth Pattern:**\n```\nIteration 0:     445 Gaussians (initial point cloud)\nIteration 500:   1,200 Gaussians (first densification)\nIteration 1000:  2,500 Gaussians (gradual growth)\nIteration 5000:  8,000 Gaussians (stabilizing)\nIteration 10000: 8,802 Gaussians (final) ✅\n\nvs.\n\nIteration 0:     445 Gaussians\nIteration 5000:  73,548 Gaussians\nIteration 8000:  272,112 Gaussians\nIteration 8500:  423,821 Gaussians → GPU CRASH ❌\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. Densification Activity - GROWTH HEALTH\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- Split/clone events concentrated in first 60% of training\n- Pruning rate 10-30% of split+clone rate\n- Densification tapering off in final 30% of training\n- grad_p90 staying between 0.0001 and 0.5\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Zero densification events (densify_split + densify_clone == 0)\n- ❌ Excessive pruning (pruned > split + cloned)\n- ❌ grad_p90 > 1.0 (gradient explosion)\n- ❌ Densification cap hit repeatedly (cap_hit warnings)\n\n**Healthy Densification Pattern:**\n```\nIterations 0-3000:    Heavy split/clone activity\nIterations 3000-7000: Moderate activity\nIterations 7000+:     Minimal activity (refinement phase)\n\nExample stats at iteration 5000:\ndensify_split: 1200\ndensify_clone: 800\ndensify_prune: 300  ✅ (15% prune rate)\ngrad_p90: 0.0521    ✅ (reasonable gradients)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 6. Gaussian Health Metrics - QUALITY INDICATORS\n\n**Anisotropy (Aspect Ratio):**\n- aniso_median: 1.5-4.0 (reasonable elongation)\n- aniso_p90: 2.0-7.0 (some needles OK)\n- aniso_max: <10.0 (no extreme needles)\n\n**Opacity Distribution:**\n- opacity_median: 0.3-0.8 (useful range)\n- opacity_low_pct: 10-40% (some weak Gaussians OK for blending)\n\n**Scale Distribution:**\n- scale_median: 0.01-0.5 (scene-dependent, should be stable)\n- scale_median should not drift >2× during final 50% of training\n\n**Gradient Magnitudes:**\n- pos_grad_median: 0.00001-0.001 (decreasing over time)\n- scale_grad_median: 0.00001-0.001\n- rot_grad_median: 0.00001-0.001\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ aniso_max > 20.0 (severe needle formation)\n- ❌ opacity_median < 0.1 (all Gaussians too transparent)\n- ❌ opacity_low_pct > 70% (most Gaussians ineffective)\n- ❌ Gradient medians >0.01 after iteration 5000 (not converging)\n\n---\n\n## Optimizer Stability Metrics\n\n### 7. Background Color - OPTIMIZATION HEALTH\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- All RGB components in range [0.0, 1.0]\n- Stable in final 30% of training (change < 0.1 per component)\n- Background not pure black [0,0,0] or pure white [1,1,1]\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Any component < 0.0 or > 1.0 (unphysical, optimizer diverged)\n- ❌ Drifting rapidly (>0.3 change per 1000 iterations)\n- ❌ Extreme values even if in range (e.g., [0.001, 0.001, 0.001])\n\n**Example:**\n```\nIteration 1000: bg=(0.46, 0.55, 0.47) ✅\nIteration 3000: bg=(0.48, 0.54, 0.46) ✅ (stable)\n\nvs.\n\nIteration 1000: bg=(0.46, 0.55, 0.47) ✅\nIteration 2000: bg=(0.86, 1.00, 0.83) ⚠️ (approaching limit)\nIteration 3000: bg=(0.95, 1.15, 0.84) ❌ (GREEN > 1.0 - DIVERGED)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 8. Gradient Statistics - CONVERGENCE HEALTH\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- grad_p50 decreasing over training\n- grad_p90 staying <0.5 throughout\n- Final grad_p50 <0.0001 (approaching convergence)\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ grad_p50 increasing after iteration 3000\n- ❌ grad_p90 >1.0 at any point (explosion)\n- ❌ grad_p50 >0.001 at final iteration (not converged)\n\n**Healthy Gradient Evolution:**\n```\nIteration 1000:  grad_p50=0.0005, grad_p90=0.05\nIteration 5000:  grad_p50=0.0002, grad_p90=0.02  ✅ (decreasing)\nIteration 10000: grad_p50=0.00005, grad_p90=0.005 ✅ (converged)\n\nvs.\n\nIteration 5000:  grad_p50=0.0007, grad_p90=0.0007\nIteration 6000:  grad_p50=0.0023, grad_p90=0.2626\nIteration 7000:  grad_p50=0.0087, grad_p90=0.7824  ❌ (exploding)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Checkpoint-Based Quality Gates\n\n### Iteration 1000 - Early Validation\n\n**PASS Requirements:**\n- ✅ PSNR improved by ≥0.3 dB OR PSNR >11 dB\n- ✅ Loss decreased from initial\n- ✅ Background RGB all <1.0\n- ✅ Gaussians 1.5× to 5× initial count\n- ✅ Visual: Scene recognizable in rendered test view\n\n**ABORT if:**\n- ❌ PSNR decreased >1.0 dB\n- ❌ File size <50 KB (solid color)\n- ❌ Background component >1.0\n\n---\n\n### Iteration 3000 - Stability Check\n\n**PASS Requirements:**\n- ✅ PSNR improved by ≥0.8 dB from initial\n- ✅ PSNR not decreasing (iteration 3000 ≥ iteration 2000)\n- ✅ Gaussians 2× to 10× initial count\n- ✅ grad_p90 <0.5\n- ✅ Visual: Clear scene details visible\n\n**ABORT if:**\n- ❌ PSNR degrading for 2+ consecutive checkpoints\n- ❌ Gaussians >50K for <100 image datasets\n- ❌ Any component of background >1.0\n\n---\n\n### Iteration 5000-8000 - Densification Risk Window\n\n**PASS Requirements:**\n- ✅ Gaussian count growth rate slowing (<20% per 1000 iterations)\n- ✅ grad_p90 stable or decreasing\n- ✅ No GPU crashes or buffer overflows\n- ✅ PSNR continuing to improve or stable\n\n**MONITOR:**\n- Densification cap not being hit repeatedly\n- File sizes staying >150 KB\n- Loss not increasing\n\n---\n\n### Final Iteration - Completion Validation\n\n**HIGH QUALITY Requirements:**\n- ✅ PSNR improvement ≥1.5 dB (dataset-dependent targets above)\n- ✅ Final PSNR ≥12 dB\n- ✅ Visual quality: Recognizable, detailed scene\n- ✅ File size ≥200 KB\n- ✅ Gaussian count in healthy range (see section 4)\n- ✅ Background RGB all in [0.0, 1.0]\n- ✅ grad_p50 <0.0001 (converged)\n\n**ACCEPTABLE Quality (may require retraining):**\n- ⚠️ PSNR improvement +0.5 to +1.5 dB\n- ⚠️ Final PSNR 10-12 dB\n- ⚠️ File size 100-200 KB\n- ⚠️ Some minor artifacts but scene recognizable\n\n**FAILED Training:**\n- ❌ PSNR decreased or improved <0.5 dB\n- ❌ Final PSNR <10 dB\n- ❌ File size <100 KB\n- ❌ Solid color or unrecognizable rendering\n- ❌ Background RGB outside [0.0, 1.0]\n- ❌ Severe visual artifacts\n\n---\n\n## Configuration-Specific Success Criteria\n\n### Micro Preset (20 images, 2000 iterations)\n- **Target PSNR improvement:** +1.5 to +3.0 dB\n- **Expected final Gaussians:** 8,000-12,000\n- **Expected duration:** ~5-10 minutes\n- **File size target:** 200-300 KB\n\n### Onehour Preset (75 images, 10K iterations)\n- **Target PSNR improvement:** +2.5 to +5.0 dB\n- **Expected final Gaussians:** 8,000-15,000\n- **Expected duration:** ~60 minutes\n- **File size target:** 250-400 KB\n\n### Full Preset (100 images, 30K iterations)\n- **Target PSNR improvement:** +3.0 to +6.0 dB\n- **Expected final Gaussians:** 25,000-50,000\n- **Expected duration:** ~5-7 hours\n- **File size target:** 300-500 KB\n\n---\n\n## Early Abort Conditions\n\nTraining should be **aborted immediately** if ANY of these occur:\n\n1. **Solid Color Failure:** File size <50 KB at iteration 1000\n2. **Background Divergence:** Any RGB component outside [0.0, 1.0]\n3. **PSNR Collapse:** PSNR decreased >2.0 dB from peak\n4. **Gradient Explosion:** grad_p90 >1.0\n5. **Gaussian Explosion:** Growing >50% per 1000 iterations after iteration 3000\n6. **GPU Crash:** Buffer overflow or memory errors\n7. **Persistent Degradation:** PSNR decreasing for 3+ consecutive checkpoints\n\n**Rationale:** These conditions indicate fundamental optimization failure that will not recover. Aborting early saves compute time.\n\n---\n\n## Post-Training Validation\n\nAfter training completes, perform final validation:\n\n### 1. Model File Validation\n```bash\n# Check model was saved\nls -lh runs/YOUR_RUN/model.gs\n# Should be 1-10 MB depending on Gaussian count\n\n# Verify metadata\n# - num_gaussians matches final count\n# - training_psnr matches final PSNR\n# - bounds_min/max are reasonable (not NaN/Inf)\n```\n\n### 2. Metrics CSV Validation\n```bash\n# Check CSV has all iterations\nwc -l runs/YOUR_RUN/metrics.csv\n# Should match: (iters / log_interval) + 1 header\n\n# Verify no NaN values\ngrep -i nan runs/YOUR_RUN/metrics.csv\n# Should return nothing\n\n# Check PSNR trend\ntail -20 runs/YOUR_RUN/metrics.csv | awk -F, '{print $3}'\n# Should show stable or increasing values\n```\n\n### 3. Visual Validation\n```bash\n# Review final test view\nopen runs/YOUR_RUN/m8_test_view_rendered.png\nopen runs/YOUR_RUN/m8_test_view_target.png\n\n# Check for:\n# - Scene recognizable ✅\n# - Major features present ✅\n# - Reasonable colors ✅\n# - No massive artifacts ✅\n```\n\n### 4. Viewer Validation (if available)\n```bash\n# Load model in viewer\nsugar-viewer runs/YOUR_RUN/model.gs\n\n# Verify:\n# - Model loads without errors ✅\n# - Novel views render correctly ✅\n# - No major holes or artifacts ✅\n# - Quality matches static renders ✅\n```\n\n---\n\n## Summary Checklist\n\nUse this checklist to quickly determine training success:\n\n**✅ HIGH QUALITY Run:**\n- [ ] PSNR improved ≥1.5 dB\n- [ ] Final PSNR ≥12 dB\n- [ ] Background RGB all in [0.0, 1.0]\n- [ ] Gaussian count in healthy range\n- [ ] File size ≥200 KB\n- [ ] Scene recognizable with good detail\n- [ ] No gradient explosions\n- [ ] Model file saved successfully\n\n**⚠️ ACCEPTABLE Run (consider retraining):**\n- [ ] PSNR improved 0.5-1.5 dB\n- [ ] Some minor quality issues\n- [ ] File size 100-200 KB\n- [ ] Scene recognizable but artifacts present\n\n**❌ FAILED Run (must retrain):**\n- [ ] PSNR decreased or <0.5 dB improvement\n- [ ] Background RGB outside [0.0, 1.0]\n- [ ] Solid color or unrecognizable rendering\n- [ ] File size <100 KB\n- [ ] Severe artifacts or crashes\n\n---\n\n## Automated Quality Assessment (Future)\n\nRecommended thresholds for automated pass/fail:\n\n```python\ndef assess_training_quality(metrics_csv, final_png):\n    \"\"\"\n    Returns: 'HIGH_QUALITY' | 'ACCEPTABLE' | 'FAILED'\n    \"\"\"\n    initial_psnr = metrics_csv.loc[0, 'psnr']\n    final_psnr = metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'psnr']\n    improvement = final_psnr - initial_psnr\n\n    bg_max = max(abs(metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'bg_r']),\n                 abs(metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'bg_g']),\n                 abs(metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'bg_b']))\n\n    file_size_kb = os.path.getsize(final_png) / 1024\n\n    # FAILED checks\n    if improvement < 0.5:\n        return 'FAILED'\n    if bg_max > 1.0:\n        return 'FAILED'\n    if file_size_kb < 100:\n        return 'FAILED'\n    if final_psnr < 10:\n        return 'FAILED'\n\n    # HIGH_QUALITY checks\n    if improvement >= 1.5 and final_psnr >= 12 and file_size_kb >= 200:\n        return 'HIGH_QUALITY'\n\n    # Otherwise ACCEPTABLE\n    return 'ACCEPTABLE'\n```\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n- **Metrics Definition:** src/optim/trainer.rs:43-132 (CsvLogger, GaussianStats)\n- **Densification Logic:** src/optim/trainer.rs:1031-1257 (densify_and_prune)\n- **Training Loop:** src/optim/trainer.rs:1266+ (train_multiview_color_only)\n- **Known Issues:** docs/QUALITY.md (troubleshooting guide)\n\n---\n\n**Document Version:** 1.0\n**Last Updated:** 2025-01-06\n**Based on:** Analysis of metrics.csv output, QUALITY.md troubleshooting, and training loop implementation\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":986,"cache_read_input_tokens":63963,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":986,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5290,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5ccea354-2c1e-49fe-a1f8-8b9d354dc6ee","uuid":"a59770e7-1306-4ef2-9f59-e9ee33380e33"}

PSNR (Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio) - PRIMARY INDICATOR\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- **Minimum improvement:** +1.0 dB from initial to final PSNR\n- **Target improvement by dataset size:**\n  - 20-40 images: +1.5 to +3.0 dB\n  - 50-75 images: +2.5 to +5.0 dB\n  - 100 images: +3.0 to +6.0 dB\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ PSNR degrading over time (final < initial)\n- ❌ PSNR flat for >3000 consecutive iterations\n- ❌ PSNR < 10 dB at any checkpoint after iteration 1000\n\n**Measurement Points:**\n- Initial: Iteration 1-100 (before densification)\n- Checkpoint: Every val_interval iterations\n- Final: Last iteration\n\n**Example Targets:**\n```\nInitial PSNR: 10.44 dB\nFinal PSNR:   14.51 dB  ✅ (+4.08 dB improvement)\n\nInitial PSNR: 12.81 dB\nFinal PSNR:   5.42 dB   ❌ (-7.39 dB degradation - FAILURE)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2. Training Loss - CONVERGENCE INDICATOR\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- Loss decreasing over the first 50% of training\n- Loss stabilizing or slowly decreasing in final 25% of training\n- Final loss < 0.5 for L2 loss\n- Final loss < 0.3 for L1+DSSIM loss\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Loss increasing for >1000 consecutive iterations\n- ❌ Loss > 1.0 after iteration 5000\n- ❌ Loss fluctuating wildly (variance > 0.2 over 100 iterations)\n\n**Measurement:**\n- Track moving average over 100 iterations\n- Compare iteration N to iteration N-1000\n\n---\n\n### 3. Visual Quality - PERCEPTUAL VALIDATION\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- Rendered test views show recognizable scene structure\n- Major geometric features clearly visible\n- Colors reasonably accurate (no solid color failures)\n- Minimal black artifacts or floating splats\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Solid color renderings (file size <50 KB)\n- ❌ Completely black or white images\n- ❌ Unrecognizable scene (no correspondence to input views)\n- ❌ Severe artifacts (large black regions, extreme noise)\n\n**File Size Heuristics:**\n```\n< 50 KB:     Solid color failure ❌\n50-150 KB:   Very poor quality ⚠️\n150-250 KB:  Marginal quality ⚠️\n250-400 KB:  Good quality ✅\n> 400 KB:    Excellent detail ✅\n```\n\n**Validation:**\n- Check rendered test views at iterations 1000, 3000, 5000, final\n- Compare rendered vs target side-by-side\n- Verify scene is recognizable by iteration 3000\n\n---\n\n## Gaussian Population Metrics\n\n### 4. Gaussian Count - CAPACITY MANAGEMENT\n\n**Success Criteria by Dataset Size:**\n\n**20-40 images:**\n- Initial: 200-1,000 Gaussians\n- Final: 5,000-15,000 Gaussians\n- Growth ratio: 5-15×\n\n**50-75 images:**\n- Initial: 400-2,000 Gaussians\n- Final: 8,000-20,000 Gaussians\n- Growth ratio: 4-10×\n\n**100 images:**\n- Initial: 500-3,000 Gaussians\n- Final: 15,000-50,000 Gaussians\n- Growth ratio: 5-15×\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Zero growth (final == initial): Densification not working\n- ❌ Explosive growth (>10× in <3000 iterations): Runaway densification\n- ❌ Exceeding GPU capacity (>400K Gaussians on Metal GPU)\n- ❌ >100K Gaussians for datasets <100 images\n\n**Healthy Growth Pattern:**\n```\nIteration 0:     445 Gaussians (initial point cloud)\nIteration 500:   1,200 Gaussians (first densification)\nIteration 1000:  2,500 Gaussians (gradual growth)\nIteration 5000:  8,000 Gaussians (stabilizing)\nIteration 10000: 8,802 Gaussians (final) ✅\n\nvs.\n\nIteration 0:     445 Gaussians\nIteration 5000:  73,548 Gaussians\nIteration 8000:  272,112 Gaussians\nIteration 8500:  423,821 Gaussians → GPU CRASH ❌\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. Densification Activity - GROWTH HEALTH\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- Split/clone events concentrated in first 60% of training\n- Pruning rate 10-30% of split+clone rate\n- Densification tapering off in final 30% of training\n- grad_p90 staying between 0.0001 and 0.5\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Zero densification events (densify_split + densify_clone == 0)\n- ❌ Excessive pruning (pruned > split + cloned)\n- ❌ grad_p90 > 1.0 (gradient explosion)\n- ❌ Densification cap hit repeatedly (cap_hit warnings)\n\n**Healthy Densification Pattern:**\n```\nIterations 0-3000:    Heavy split/clone activity\nIterations 3000-7000: Moderate activity\nIterations 7000+:     Minimal activity (refinement phase)\n\nExample stats at iteration 5000:\ndensify_split: 1200\ndensify_clone: 800\ndensify_prune: 300  ✅ (15% prune rate)\ngrad_p90: 0.0521    ✅ (reasonable gradients)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 6. Gaussian Health Metrics - QUALITY INDICATORS\n\n**Anisotropy (Aspect Ratio):**\n- aniso_median: 1.5-4.0 (reasonable elongation)\n- aniso_p90: 2.0-7.0 (some needles OK)\n- aniso_max: <10.0 (no extreme needles)\n\n**Opacity Distribution:**\n- opacity_median: 0.3-0.8 (useful range)\n- opacity_low_pct: 10-40% (some weak Gaussians OK for blending)\n\n**Scale Distribution:**\n- scale_median: 0.01-0.5 (scene-dependent, should be stable)\n- scale_median should not drift >2× during final 50% of training\n\n**Gradient Magnitudes:**\n- pos_grad_median: 0.00001-0.001 (decreasing over time)\n- scale_grad_median: 0.00001-0.001\n- rot_grad_median: 0.00001-0.001\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ aniso_max > 20.0 (severe needle formation)\n- ❌ opacity_median < 0.1 (all Gaussians too transparent)\n- ❌ opacity_low_pct > 70% (most Gaussians ineffective)\n- ❌ Gradient medians >0.01 after iteration 5000 (not converging)\n\n---\n\n## Optimizer Stability Metrics\n\n### 7. Background Color - OPTIMIZATION HEALTH\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- All RGB components in range [0.0, 1.0]\n- Stable in final 30% of training (change < 0.1 per component)\n- Background not pure black [0,0,0] or pure white [1,1,1]\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ Any component < 0.0 or > 1.0 (unphysical, optimizer diverged)\n- ❌ Drifting rapidly (>0.3 change per 1000 iterations)\n- ❌ Extreme values even if in range (e.g., [0.001, 0.001, 0.001])\n\n**Example:**\n```\nIteration 1000: bg=(0.46, 0.55, 0.47) ✅\nIteration 3000: bg=(0.48, 0.54, 0.46) ✅ (stable)\n\nvs.\n\nIteration 1000: bg=(0.46, 0.55, 0.47) ✅\nIteration 2000: bg=(0.86, 1.00, 0.83) ⚠️ (approaching limit)\nIteration 3000: bg=(0.95, 1.15, 0.84) ❌ (GREEN > 1.0 - DIVERGED)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 8. Gradient Statistics - CONVERGENCE HEALTH\n\n**Success Criteria:**\n- grad_p50 decreasing over training\n- grad_p90 staying <0.5 throughout\n- Final grad_p50 <0.0001 (approaching convergence)\n\n**Failure Indicators:**\n- ❌ grad_p50 increasing after iteration 3000\n- ❌ grad_p90 >1.0 at any point (explosion)\n- ❌ grad_p50 >0.001 at final iteration (not converged)\n\n**Healthy Gradient Evolution:**\n```\nIteration 1000:  grad_p50=0.0005, grad_p90=0.05\nIteration 5000:  grad_p50=0.0002, grad_p90=0.02  ✅ (decreasing)\nIteration 10000: grad_p50=0.00005, grad_p90=0.005 ✅ (converged)\n\nvs.\n\nIteration 5000:  grad_p50=0.0007, grad_p90=0.0007\nIteration 6000:  grad_p50=0.0023, grad_p90=0.2626\nIteration 7000:  grad_p50=0.0087, grad_p90=0.7824  ❌ (exploding)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Checkpoint-Based Quality Gates\n\n### Iteration 1000 - Early Validation\n\n**PASS Requirements:**\n- ✅ PSNR improved by ≥0.3 dB OR PSNR >11 dB\n- ✅ Loss decreased from initial\n- ✅ Background RGB all <1.0\n- ✅ Gaussians 1.5× to 5× initial count\n- ✅ Visual: Scene recognizable in rendered test view\n\n**ABORT if:**\n- ❌ PSNR decreased >1.0 dB\n- ❌ File size <50 KB (solid color)\n- ❌ Background component >1.0\n\n---\n\n### Iteration 3000 - Stability Check\n\n**PASS Requirements:**\n- ✅ PSNR improved by ≥0.8 dB from initial\n- ✅ PSNR not decreasing (iteration 3000 ≥ iteration 2000)\n- ✅ Gaussians 2× to 10× initial count\n- ✅ grad_p90 <0.5\n- ✅ Visual: Clear scene details visible\n\n**ABORT if:**\n- ❌ PSNR degrading for 2+ consecutive checkpoints\n- ❌ Gaussians >50K for <100 image datasets\n- ❌ Any component of background >1.0\n\n---\n\n### Iteration 5000-8000 - Densification Risk Window\n\n**PASS Requirements:**\n- ✅ Gaussian count growth rate slowing (<20% per 1000 iterations)\n- ✅ grad_p90 stable or decreasing\n- ✅ No GPU crashes or buffer overflows\n- ✅ PSNR continuing to improve or stable\n\n**MONITOR:**\n- Densification cap not being hit repeatedly\n- File sizes staying >150 KB\n- Loss not increasing\n\n---\n\n### Final Iteration - Completion Validation\n\n**HIGH QUALITY Requirements:**\n- ✅ PSNR improvement ≥1.5 dB (dataset-dependent targets above)\n- ✅ Final PSNR ≥12 dB\n- ✅ Visual quality: Recognizable, detailed scene\n- ✅ File size ≥200 KB\n- ✅ Gaussian count in healthy range (see section 4)\n- ✅ Background RGB all in [0.0, 1.0]\n- ✅ grad_p50 <0.0001 (converged)\n\n**ACCEPTABLE Quality (may require retraining):**\n- ⚠️ PSNR improvement +0.5 to +1.5 dB\n- ⚠️ Final PSNR 10-12 dB\n- ⚠️ File size 100-200 KB\n- ⚠️ Some minor artifacts but scene recognizable\n\n**FAILED Training:**\n- ❌ PSNR decreased or improved <0.5 dB\n- ❌ Final PSNR <10 dB\n- ❌ File size <100 KB\n- ❌ Solid color or unrecognizable rendering\n- ❌ Background RGB outside [0.0, 1.0]\n- ❌ Severe visual artifacts\n\n---\n\n## Configuration-Specific Success Criteria\n\n### Micro Preset (20 images, 2000 iterations)\n- **Target PSNR improvement:** +1.5 to +3.0 dB\n- **Expected final Gaussians:** 8,000-12,000\n- **Expected duration:** ~5-10 minutes\n- **File size target:** 200-300 KB\n\n### Onehour Preset (75 images, 10K iterations)\n- **Target PSNR improvement:** +2.5 to +5.0 dB\n- **Expected final Gaussians:** 8,000-15,000\n- **Expected duration:** ~60 minutes\n- **File size target:** 250-400 KB\n\n### Full Preset (100 images, 30K iterations)\n- **Target PSNR improvement:** +3.0 to +6.0 dB\n- **Expected final Gaussians:** 25,000-50,000\n- **Expected duration:** ~5-7 hours\n- **File size target:** 300-500 KB\n\n---\n\n## Early Abort Conditions\n\nTraining should be **aborted immediately** if ANY of these occur:\n\n1. **Solid Color Failure:** File size <50 KB at iteration 1000\n2. **Background Divergence:** Any RGB component outside [0.0, 1.0]\n3. **PSNR Collapse:** PSNR decreased >2.0 dB from peak\n4. **Gradient Explosion:** grad_p90 >1.0\n5. **Gaussian Explosion:** Growing >50% per 1000 iterations after iteration 3000\n6. **GPU Crash:** Buffer overflow or memory errors\n7. **Persistent Degradation:** PSNR decreasing for 3+ consecutive checkpoints\n\n**Rationale:** These conditions indicate fundamental optimization failure that will not recover. Aborting early saves compute time.\n\n---\n\n## Post-Training Validation\n\nAfter training completes, perform final validation:\n\n### 1. Model File Validation\n```bash\n# Check model was saved\nls -lh runs/YOUR_RUN/model.gs\n# Should be 1-10 MB depending on Gaussian count\n\n# Verify metadata\n# - num_gaussians matches final count\n# - training_psnr matches final PSNR\n# - bounds_min/max are reasonable (not NaN/Inf)\n```\n\n### 2. Metrics CSV Validation\n```bash\n# Check CSV has all iterations\nwc -l runs/YOUR_RUN/metrics.csv\n# Should match: (iters / log_interval) + 1 header\n\n# Verify no NaN values\ngrep -i nan runs/YOUR_RUN/metrics.csv\n# Should return nothing\n\n# Check PSNR trend\ntail -20 runs/YOUR_RUN/metrics.csv | awk -F, '{print $3}'\n# Should show stable or increasing values\n```\n\n### 3. Visual Validation\n```bash\n# Review final test view\nopen runs/YOUR_RUN/m8_test_view_rendered.png\nopen runs/YOUR_RUN/m8_test_view_target.png\n\n# Check for:\n# - Scene recognizable ✅\n# - Major features present ✅\n# - Reasonable colors ✅\n# - No massive artifacts ✅\n```\n\n### 4. Viewer Validation (if available)\n```bash\n# Load model in viewer\nsugar-viewer runs/YOUR_RUN/model.gs\n\n# Verify:\n# - Model loads without errors ✅\n# - Novel views render correctly ✅\n# - No major holes or artifacts ✅\n# - Quality matches static renders ✅\n```\n\n---\n\n## Summary Checklist\n\nUse this checklist to quickly determine training success:\n\n**✅ HIGH QUALITY Run:**\n- [ ] PSNR improved ≥1.5 dB\n- [ ] Final PSNR ≥12 dB\n- [ ] Background RGB all in [0.0, 1.0]\n- [ ] Gaussian count in healthy range\n- [ ] File size ≥200 KB\n- [ ] Scene recognizable with good detail\n- [ ] No gradient explosions\n- [ ] Model file saved successfully\n\n**⚠️ ACCEPTABLE Run (consider retraining):**\n- [ ] PSNR improved 0.5-1.5 dB\n- [ ] Some minor quality issues\n- [ ] File size 100-200 KB\n- [ ] Scene recognizable but artifacts present\n\n**❌ FAILED Run (must retrain):**\n- [ ] PSNR decreased or <0.5 dB improvement\n- [ ] Background RGB outside [0.0, 1.0]\n- [ ] Solid color or unrecognizable rendering\n- [ ] File size <100 KB\n- [ ] Severe artifacts or crashes\n\n---\n\n## Automated Quality Assessment (Future)\n\nRecommended thresholds for automated pass/fail:\n\n```python\ndef assess_training_quality(metrics_csv, final_png):\n    \"\"\"\n    Returns: 'HIGH_QUALITY' | 'ACCEPTABLE' | 'FAILED'\n    \"\"\"\n    initial_psnr = metrics_csv.loc[0, 'psnr']\n    final_psnr = metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'psnr']\n    improvement = final_psnr - initial_psnr\n\n    bg_max = max(abs(metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'bg_r']),\n                 abs(metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'bg_g']),\n                 abs(metrics_csv.loc[-1, 'bg_b']))\n\n    file_size_kb = os.path.getsize(final_png) / 1024\n\n    # FAILED checks\n    if improvement < 0.5:\n        return 'FAILED'\n    if bg_max > 1.0:\n        return 'FAILED'\n    if file_size_kb < 100:\n        return 'FAILED'\n    if final_psnr < 10:\n        return 'FAILED'\n\n    # HIGH_QUALITY checks\n    if improvement >= 1.5 and final_psnr >= 12 and file_size_kb >= 200:\n        return 'HIGH_QUALITY'\n\n    # Otherwise ACCEPTABLE\n    return 'ACCEPTABLE'\n```\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n- **Metrics Definition:** src/optim/trainer.rs:43-132 (CsvLogger, GaussianStats)\n- **Densification Logic:** src/optim/trainer.rs:1031-1257 (densify_and_prune)\n- **Training Loop:** src/optim/trainer.rs:1266+ (train_multiview_color_only)\n- **Known Issues:** docs/QUALITY.md (troubleshooting guide)\n\n---\n\n**Document Version:** 1.0\n**Last Updated:** 2025-01-06\n**Based on:** Analysis of metrics.csv output, QUALITY.md troubleshooting, and training loop implementation\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
